# Skills · V · part 69

872 entries, voice-audition to voice-observation. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **voice-audition**: VoiceAudition -- the casting director for your AI voice agent. Interviews you about your needs, searches 697+ voices across 9+ TTS providers (6 commercial + 3 open source) using semantic search, runs use-case-specific auditions, compares API vs self-hosted costs, and delivers a ranked scorecard with ready-to-use config. 645+ voices enriched with LLM-generated descriptions, traits, and tags. Web UI available at localhost:5173 for browsing and TTS preview. - **voice-auth**: Writing voice authentication and AI-smell elimination skill. Activates when the user wants AI-generated writing to sound like them specifically — not generic, not polished-AI, not corporate bland. Analyzes a writing sample to fingerprint sentence length, vocabulary density, structural habits, punctuation patterns, and filler tendencies. Generates and rewrites content matching that fingerprint. Kills AI clichés, passive construction, hollow openers, and corporate hedges. Iterates by adjusting the voice model, not just the output. Use when user says: make this sound like me, remove the AI smell… ([Sandeeprdy1729/skills](https://github.com/Sandeeprdy1729/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/voice-auth) · ★3)

Triggers: 비즈니스 톤, business voice, 격식 있는 톤, 동료 공유용 재작성, formal rewrite, 보고서 톤, 발표자료 톤. Maintains a sociolinguistic voice profile that agents read BEFORE drafting (style guide that shapes what gets written) AND self-run AFTER drafting (contamination linter), plus a learning loop that improves the profile from human revision patterns. Both the pre-draft read and the post-draft check are first-class uses — voice-check is not a post-only tool. Run /voice-check setup to create a profile from writing samples. Run /voice-check learn after a revision session to update the profile. ([disabledbydesign/cyborg-methodologies](https://github.com/disabledbydesign/cyborg-methodologies/tree/HEAD/voice-check) · ★1)
- **voice-check**: Fast pass over a draft for banned phrases, AI-tells, em-dash overuse, passive voice, throat-clearing. Returns a line-anchored hit list. Run before /critic or when a draft may have drifted from voice. ([iammatthias/styx](https://github.com/iammatthias/styx/tree/HEAD/skills/voice-check) · ★1)
- **voice-check**: Аудит русскоязычного черновика на соответствие голосу автора и на следы AI-генерации. Используй, когда нужно проверить статью перед публикацией, когда спрашивают «звучит ли это как я», «палится ли текст на ИИ», «что тут выдаёт нейросеть», или когда готовится материал для Хабра, vc.ru, сайта, Telegram. НЕ переписывает текст: возвращает таблицу мест с цитатами и по два варианта правки. Правит автор. ([maslennikov-ig/articles](https://github.com/maslennikov-ig/articles/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/voice-check) · ★1)
- **voice-check**: Optional evaluation of the user's voice pack. Holds out some of their real messages, has the plugin write each one from a content-only summary, then builds a blind-test page in two parts (pairs: pick your real message; lineups: four messages, three yours, spot the one you didn't write) and scores stylometric closeness plus an LLM judge on both. Use when the user asks "how good is my voice pack", "test it", "does it sound like me", or wants a blind test. ([ScottBull/write-like-me](https://github.com/ScottBull/write-like-me/tree/HEAD/skills/voice-check) · ★1)
- **voice-check**: Use before producing anything the human will send or publish under their own name — emails, messages, posts, proposals, bios, newsletters, landing page copy, talks, or community announcements. Also use when they say a draft does not sound like them, or sounds like AI. ([seyekuyinu/integral-ai-commons](https://github.com/seyekuyinu/integral-ai-commons/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/voice-check) · ★1)
- **voice-check**: Run the full after-draft screen on a finished or near-final draft: determine its register/voice, walk the ai-tells blocklist (Layer 0) with register-sensitive em-dash and rhetorical-question caps, run voice-critic conditioned on the target voice's exemplar bank, optionally run an AI-detector pass, then return a findings list and offer to apply fixes. Use when user says 'voice-check', 'check this draft', 'run the screen on this', 'final voice pass', 'does this sound like me and pass the tells', 'after-draft check', or '/voice-check'. Do NOT use for a voice-only pass with no tells screen (use /… ([anup-malani/fertility-review-causes](https://github.com/anup-malani/fertility-review-causes/tree/HEAD/voice-stack/core/skills/voice-check))
- **voice-check**: Validate a draft against rishabh's voice rules. Returns PASS or FAIL with specific diagnoses. The final gate before any draft reaches telegram. Every content-producing skill calls this before emitting a draft. Stateless — caller decides what to do with the verdict. ([balabommablock-cpu/aria-engine](https://github.com/balabommablock-cpu/aria-engine/tree/HEAD/skills/voice-check))
- **voice-check**: Check that a post preserves the author's meaning and voice, serves its reader, has evidence and fits its platform. Run after humanizer. ([dianadobra09/content-strategist](https://github.com/dianadobra09/content-strategist/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/voice-check))
- **voice-check**: Audit a Capable Wealth draft against Jared's voice profile — the Voice Alignment, Audience Specificity, and Pull Signal Design gates — flagging the exact lines that miss and suggesting rewrites in his voice. Composes as a verify Step 6.5 gate (wiring in P08). ([Kohlex/autopilot-plugin](https://github.com/Kohlex/autopilot-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/voice-check))
- **voice-check**: Reviews a draft against the voice rules in AGENTS.md before it ships. Use whenever external copy is finished or nearly finished (email, post, slide, one-pager, README, application) and before anything leaves the team. ([Lioserious/claude-brain-template](https://github.com/Lioserious/claude-brain-template/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/voice-check))
- **voice-check**: Checks a draft against a written voice profile and says where it drifted - six dimensions scored 1 to 10 (tone, vocabulary, rhythm, structure, personality, angle), each low score quoting the draft line next to the profile rule it breaks, measured rhythm drift from a script, and a slop floor from the no-ai-slop gate. Decides whether a change is drift or deliberate evolution. Use when a draft was written for someone else's voice, a ghostwriter or model wrote it, someone asks "does this sound like me", or before publishing under a person's name. - **voice-check**: Detect-only - scores a draft against the user's writing-voice profile (length, opener/sign-off class, bans, caricature, unbriefed identifiers). Use when the user asks does this sound or read like me, would I have written this, is this how I usually write, check or score a draft against my voice, or run the self-test. Not for rewriting, profile building, voice recordings, or AI-slop linting. ([Paldom/personal-tone](https://github.com/Paldom/personal-tone/tree/HEAD/skills/voice-check))

- **voice-check**: Use when Alex wants to know if writing sounds like him — "does this sound like me?", "check my voice", "review this for tone", or when reviewing AI-drafted content before sending. - **voice-check**: Scans a document for AI writing tells, including em dashes, hedging, puffery, AI vocabulary clusters, promotional tone, bridge phrases, and leaked markup artifacts. Layers global rules with per-repo supplements found at .claude/voice-check.md walking up from the target file.
